[[SliceShade.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[use_lighting_effect]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.SliceShade.use_lighting_effect "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Use lighting effect.

    When set to [[`True`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/constants.html#True "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
    .external}, the lighting effect may be selected with the
    [[`SliceEffects.lighting_effect`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.SliceEffects.lighting_effect "tecplot.plot.SliceEffects.lighting_effect"){.reference
    .internal} attribute.

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    Setting [[`SliceShade.use_lighting_effect`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.SliceShade.use_lighting_effect "tecplot.plot.SliceShade.use_lighting_effect"){.reference
    .internal} will also set the same value for
    [[`SliceContour.use_lighting_effect`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.SliceContour.use_lighting_effect "tecplot.plot.SliceContour.use_lighting_effect"){.reference
    .internal}, and vice-versa.
    :::

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> plot.slice(0).shade.use_lighting_effect = True
        >>> plot.slice(0).effects.lighting_effect = LightingEffect.Paneled
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`bool`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}
:::

::: {#slicevector .section}
### [SliceVector](#id127){.toc-backref role="doc-backlink"}[¶](#slicevector "Link to this heading"){.headerlink}

*[class]{.pre}[ ]{.w}*[[tecplot.plot.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[SliceVector]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[slice_group]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/slice.html#SliceVector){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.SliceVector "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Vector attributes of the slice group.

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        from os import path
        import tecplot as tp
        from tecplot.constant import *

        examples_dir = tp.session.tecplot_examples_directory()
        datafile = path.join(examples_dir, 'SimpleData', 'DuctFlow.plt')
        dataset = tp.data.load_tecplot(datafile)

        plot = tp.active_frame().plot()

        plot.show_slices = True
        slice_0 = plot.slice(0)

        plot.contour(0).variable = dataset.variable('T(K)')

        # Vector variables must be assigned before displaying
        vectors = plot.vector
        vectors.u_variable = dataset.variable('U(M/S)')
        vectors.v_variable = dataset.variable('V(M/S)')
        vectors.w_variable = dataset.variable('W(M/S)')

        slice_vector = plot.slice(0).vector
        slice_vector.show = True
        slice_vector.vector_type = VectorType.MidAtPoint
        slice_vector.color = Color.BluePurple

        slice_0.effects.use_translucency = True
        slice_0.effects.surface_translucency = 30

        # ensure consistent output between interactive (connected) and batch
        plot.contour(0).levels.reset_to_nice()

        tp.export.save_png('slice_vector.png', 600, supersample=3)
    :::
    ::::

    <figure class="align-default" style="width: 300px">
    <a href="../_images/slice_vector.png"
    class="reference internal image-reference"><img
    src="../_images/slice_vector.png" style="width: 300px;"
    alt="../_images/slice_vector.png" /></a>
    </figure>

      [[`color`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.SliceVector.color "tecplot.plot.SliceVector.color"){.reference .internal}                                 Set slice vector color.
      [[`is_tangent`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.SliceVector.is_tangent "tecplot.plot.SliceVector.is_tangent"){.reference .internal}                  Use tangent vectors for slices.
      [[`line_thickness`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.SliceVector.line_thickness "tecplot.plot.SliceVector.line_thickness"){.reference .internal}      Vector line thickness as a percentage of the frame height.
      [[`show`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.SliceVector.show "tecplot.plot.SliceVector.show"){.reference .internal}                                    Show vectors on slices.

### [Streamtraces](#id128){.toc-backref role="doc-backlink"}[¶](#id9 "Link to this heading"){.headerlink}

*[class]{.pre}[ ]{.w}*[[tecplot.plot.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[Streamtraces]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[plot]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#Streamtraces){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Streamtrace attributes for the plot.

    A streamtrace is the path traced by a massless particle placed at an
    arbitrary location in a steady-state vector field. Streamtraces may
    be used to illustrate the nature of the vector field flow in a
    particular region of the [[Plot]{.std
    .std-ref}](tecplot.plot.html#plot){.reference .internal}.

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    Because streamtraces are dependent upon a vector field, you must
    define vector components before creating streamtraces. However, it
    is not necessary to activate the Vector zone layer to use
    streamtraces.
    :::

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        import os
        import tecplot
        from tecplot.constant import *

        examples_dir = tecplot.session.tecplot_examples_directory()
        datafile = os.path.join(examples_dir, 'SimpleData', 'Eddy.plt')
        dataset = tecplot.data.load_tecplot(datafile)

        frame = tecplot.active_frame()
        frame.plot_type = tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D

        plot = frame.plot()
        plot.fieldmap(0).surfaces.surfaces_to_plot = SurfacesToPlot.BoundaryFaces
        plot.show_mesh = True
        plot.show_shade = False

        plot.vector.u_variable_index = 4
        plot.vector.v_variable_index = 5
        plot.vector.w_variable_index = 6
        plot.show_streamtraces = True

        streamtraces = plot.streamtraces
        streamtraces.color = Color.Blue

        streamtraces.show_arrows = True
        streamtraces.arrowhead_size = 2
        streamtraces.step_size = .25
        streamtraces.line_thickness = .2
        streamtraces.max_steps = 100

        streamtraces.add_rake(start_position=(45.49, 15.32, 59.1),
                              end_position=(48.89, 53.2, 47.6),
                              stream_type=Streamtrace.SurfaceLine,
                              num_seed_points=4)


        tecplot.export.save_png('streamtrace_example.png', 600, supersample=3)
    :::
    ::::

    <figure class="align-default" style="width: 300px">
    <a href="../_images/streamtrace_example.png"
    class="reference internal image-reference"><img
    src="../_images/streamtrace_example.png" style="width: 300px;"
    alt="../_images/streamtrace_example.png" /></a>
    </figure>

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[active]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.active "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Determine if there are active streamtraces.

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    This property is read-only.
    :::

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`bool`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}. [[`True`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/constants.html#True "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external} if there are active streamtraces in the current plot
        type.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> streamtraces_are_active = plot.streamtraces.active
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`bool`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}

<!-- -->

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[add]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[seed_point]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[stream_type]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[direction]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[StreamDir.Both]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#Streamtraces.add){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.add "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Add a single streamtrace to the plot of the current frame.

    The plot type must be either [[`Cartesian2D`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian2D "tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian2D"){.reference
    .internal} or [[`Cartesian3D`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D "tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D"){.reference
    .internal}.

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    :   - **seed_point** -- (2- or 3- [[`tuple`{.xref .any .docutils
          .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#tuple "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} of [[`floats`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external}): Pass a 2-[[`tuple`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#tuple "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} of [[`float`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} for a [[`Cartesian2DFieldPlot`{.xref .any .py
          .py-class .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.plot.html#tecplot.plot.Cartesian2DFieldPlot "tecplot.plot.Cartesian2DFieldPlot"){.reference
          .internal}, or a 3-[[`tuple`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#tuple "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} of [[`float`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} for a [[`Cartesian3DFieldPlot`{.xref .any .py
          .py-class .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.plot.html#tecplot.plot.Cartesian3DFieldPlot "tecplot.plot.Cartesian3DFieldPlot"){.reference
          .internal}.

        - **stream_type** -- ([[`Streamtrace`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
          .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.ProbeObject.Streamtrace "tecplot.constant.ProbeObject.Streamtrace"){.reference
          .internal}): Type of streamtraces to add.

        - **direction** -- ([[`StreamDir`{.xref .any .py .py-class
          .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.StreamDir "tecplot.constant.StreamDir"){.reference
          .internal}, optional): Direction of propagation of the
          streamtraces being added.

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    *stream_type* is automatically set to
    [[`Streamtrace.SurfaceLine`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.Streamtrace.SurfaceLine "tecplot.constant.Streamtrace.SurfaceLine"){.reference
    .internal} if the plot type is [[`Cartesian2DFieldPlot`{.xref .any
    .py .py-class .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.plot.html#tecplot.plot.Cartesian2DFieldPlot "tecplot.plot.Cartesian2DFieldPlot"){.reference
    .internal}. The only stream type available for 2D plots is
    [[`Streamtrace.SurfaceLine`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.Streamtrace.SurfaceLine "tecplot.constant.Streamtrace.SurfaceLine"){.reference
    .internal}.
    :::

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        import os
        import tecplot
        from tecplot.constant import *
        import numpy as np

        examples_dir = tecplot.session.tecplot_examples_directory()
        datafile = os.path.join(examples_dir, 'SimpleData', 'DuctFlow.plt')
        dataset = tecplot.data.load_tecplot(datafile)

        frame = tecplot.active_frame()
        frame.plot_type = tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D

        plot = frame.plot()
        plot.contour(0).variable = dataset.variable('P(N/m2)')
        plot.contour(0).levels.reset_to_nice()
        plot.contour(0).legend.show = False

        plot.vector.u_variable = dataset.variable('U(M/S)')
        plot.vector.v_variable = dataset.variable('V(M/S)')
        plot.vector.w_variable = dataset.variable('W(M/S)')

        # Goal: create a grid of 12 stream trace ribbons
        x_slice_location = .79
        y_start = .077
        y_end = .914
        z_start = .052
        z_end = .415

        num_left_right_slices = 4  # Must be >= 2
        num_top_bottom_slices = 3  # Must be >= 2

        plot.show_streamtraces = True
        streamtraces = plot.streamtraces
        streamtraces.show_paths = True

        rod = streamtraces.rod_ribbon
        rod.width = .03
        rod.contour.show = True

        for y in np.linspace(y_start, y_end, num=num_left_right_slices):
            for z in np.linspace(z_start, z_end, num=num_top_bottom_slices):
                streamtraces.add([x_slice_location,y,z], Streamtrace.VolumeRibbon)

        tecplot.export.save_png('streamtrace_add_xyz.png', 600, supersample=3)
    :::
    ::::

    <figure class="align-default" style="width: 300px">
    <a href="../_images/streamtrace_add_xyz.png"
    class="reference internal image-reference"><img
    src="../_images/streamtrace_add_xyz.png" style="width: 300px;"
    alt="../_images/streamtrace_add_xyz.png" /></a>
    </figure>

<!-- -->

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[add_on_zone_surface]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[stream_type]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[zones]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[num_seed_points]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[10]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[direction]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[StreamDir.Both]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#Streamtraces.add_on_zone_surface){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.add_on_zone_surface "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Add streamtraces to one or more zones in a plot.

    The plot type must be either [[`Cartesian2D`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian2D "tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian2D"){.reference
    .internal} or [[`Cartesian3D`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D "tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D"){.reference
    .internal}.

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    For volume zones the streamtraces are propagated from the surfaces
    of the volume.
    :::

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    :   - **stream_type** -- ([[`Streamtrace`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
          .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.ProbeObject.Streamtrace "tecplot.constant.ProbeObject.Streamtrace"){.reference
          .internal}): Type of streamtraces to add.

        - **zones** ([[`set`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#set "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} of [[`integers`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#int "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external}, optional) -- Set of [[Zones]{.std
          .std-ref}](tecplot.data.html#data-access){.reference
          .internal} on which to add streamtraces. If [[`None`{.xref
          .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/constants.html#None "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external}, then streamtraces will be added to the currently
          active zones.

        - **num_seed_points** -- ([[`int`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#int "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external}, optional): Number of seed points for distributing
          along a rake or on defined surfaces.

        - **direction** -- ([[`StreamDir`{.xref .any .py .py-class
          .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.StreamDir "tecplot.constant.StreamDir"){.reference
          .internal}, optional): Direction of propagation of the
          streamtraces being added.

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        import os

        import tecplot
        from tecplot.constant import *

        examples_dir = tecplot.session.tecplot_examples_directory()
        datafile = os.path.join(examples_dir, 'OneraM6wing', 'OneraM6_SU2_RANS.plt')
        dataset = tecplot.data.load_tecplot(datafile)

        frame = tecplot.active_frame()
        frame.plot_type = tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D

        plot = frame.plot()

        plot.vector.u_variable_index = 4
        plot.vector.v_variable_index = 5
        plot.vector.w_variable_index = 6
        plot.show_streamtraces = True

        plot.streamtraces.add_on_zone_surface(
                    # To add streamtraces to the currently active zones,
                    # pass zones=None
                    zones=[1],  # Add streamtraces on 2nd zone only
                    stream_type=Streamtrace.SurfaceLine,
                    num_seed_points=200)

        tecplot.export.save_png('streamtrace_add_on_zone_surface.png', 600, supersample=3)
    :::
    ::::

    <figure class="align-default" style="width: 300px">
    <a href="../_images/streamtrace_add_on_zone_surface.png"
    class="reference internal image-reference"><img
    src="../_images/streamtrace_add_on_zone_surface.png"
    style="width: 300px;"
    alt="../_images/streamtrace_add_on_zone_surface.png" /></a>
    </figure>

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[add_rake]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[start_position]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[end_position]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[stream_type]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[num_seed_points]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[10]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[direction]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[StreamDir.Both]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#Streamtraces.add_rake){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.add_rake "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Add a rake of streamtraces to the plot of the current frame.

    The plot type must be either [[`Cartesian2D`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian2D "tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian2D"){.reference
    .internal} or [[`Cartesian3D`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D "tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D"){.reference
    .internal}.

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    :   - **start_position** -- (2- or 3- [[`tuple`{.xref .any .docutils
          .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#tuple "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} of [[`floats`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external}): Pass a 2-[[`tuple`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#tuple "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} of [[`float`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} for a [[`Cartesian2DFieldPlot`{.xref .any .py
          .py-class .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.plot.html#tecplot.plot.Cartesian2DFieldPlot "tecplot.plot.Cartesian2DFieldPlot"){.reference
          .internal}, or a 3-[[`tuple`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#tuple "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} of [[`float`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} for a [[`Cartesian3DFieldPlot`{.xref .any .py
          .py-class .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.plot.html#tecplot.plot.Cartesian3DFieldPlot "tecplot.plot.Cartesian3DFieldPlot"){.reference
          .internal}.

        - **end_position** -- (2- or 3- [[`tuple`{.xref .any .docutils
          .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#tuple "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} of [[`floats`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external}): Pass a 2-[[`tuple`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#tuple "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} of [[`float`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} for a [[`Cartesian2DFieldPlot`{.xref .any .py
          .py-class .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.plot.html#tecplot.plot.Cartesian2DFieldPlot "tecplot.plot.Cartesian2DFieldPlot"){.reference
          .internal}, or a 3-[[`tuple`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#tuple "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} of [[`float`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external} for a [[`Cartesian3DFieldPlot`{.xref .any .py
          .py-class .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.plot.html#tecplot.plot.Cartesian3DFieldPlot "tecplot.plot.Cartesian3DFieldPlot"){.reference
          .internal}.

        - **stream_type** -- ([[`Streamtrace`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
          .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.ProbeObject.Streamtrace "tecplot.constant.ProbeObject.Streamtrace"){.reference
          .internal}): Type of streamtraces to add.

        - **num_seed_points** -- ([[`int`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#int "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external}, optional): Number of seed points for distributing
          along a rake or on defined surfaces.

        - **direction** -- ([[`StreamDir`{.xref .any .py .py-class
          .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.StreamDir "tecplot.constant.StreamDir"){.reference
          .internal}, optional): Direction of propagation of the
          streamtraces being added.

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        import os

        import tecplot
        from tecplot.constant import *

        examples_dir = tecplot.session.tecplot_examples_directory()
        datafile = os.path.join(examples_dir, 'SimpleData', 'Eddy.plt')
        dataset = tecplot.data.load_tecplot(datafile)

        frame = tecplot.active_frame()
        frame.plot_type = tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D

        plot = frame.plot()
        plot.fieldmap(0).surfaces.surfaces_to_plot = SurfacesToPlot.BoundaryFaces
        plot.show_mesh = True
        plot.show_shade = False

        plot.vector.u_variable_index = 4
        plot.vector.v_variable_index = 5
        plot.vector.w_variable_index = 6
        plot.show_streamtraces = True

        streamtraces = plot.streamtraces
        streamtraces.add_rake(start_position=[.5, .5, .5],
                              end_position=[20, 20, 20],
                              stream_type=Streamtrace.VolumeLine)

        tecplot.export.save_png('streamtrace_add_rake.png', 600, supersample=3)
    :::
    ::::

    <figure class="align-default" style="width: 300px">

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[arrowhead_spacing]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.arrowhead_spacing "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Distance between arrowheads in terms of Y-frame units.

    For example, a value of 10 will space arrowheads approximately ten
    percent of the frame height apart from each other along each
    streamline.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> plot.streamtraces.show_arrows = True
        >>> plot.streamtraces.arrowhead_spacing = 10
    :::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`float`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[delete_all]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#Streamtraces.delete_all){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.delete_all "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Delete all streamtraces for the current plot type.

    2D and 3D streamtraces are independent of each other.

    If the plot type is [[`Cartesian2D`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian2D "tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian2D"){.reference
    .internal}, all 2D streamtraces are deleted. If the plot type is
    [[`Cartesian3D`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D "tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D"){.reference
    .internal}, all 3D streamtraces are deleted.

    Raises[:]{.colon}

    :   [**TecplotSystemError**](tecplot.exceptions.html#tecplot.exception.TecplotSystemError "tecplot.exception.TecplotSystemError"){.reference
        .internal} -- The streamtraces could not be deleted.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> plot.streamtraces.delete_all()
    :::
    ::::

<!-- -->

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[delete_range]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[range_start]{.pre}]{.n}*, *[[range_end]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#Streamtraces.delete_range){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.delete_range "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Delete a range of streamtraces.

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    :   - **range_start** -- ([[`int`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#int "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external}): 0-based start streamtrace number to delete.

        - **range_end** -- ([[`int`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#int "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external}): 0-based end streamtrace number to delete.

    Raises[:]{.colon}

    :   [**TecplotSystemError**](tecplot.exceptions.html#tecplot.exception.TecplotSystemError "tecplot.exception.TecplotSystemError"){.reference
        .internal} -- The streamtraces in the range could not be
        deleted.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> # Delete the first 100 streamtraces
        >>> plot.streamtraces.delete_range(0, 99)
    :::
    ::::

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[extract]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[concatenate]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[False]{.pre}]{.default_value}*, *[[assign_strand_ids]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[True]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#Streamtraces.extract){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.extract "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Create new zones from streamtraces

    Extracts the current streamtraces defined in this class to the
    [[`Dataset`{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.data.html#tecplot.data.Dataset "tecplot.data.Dataset"){.reference
    .internal} as one or more zones.

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    :   - **concatenate** ([[`bool`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external}, optional) -- Concatenate streamtraces into a
          single zone for each format (Surface Line, Volume Line, Volume
          Ribbon, Volume Rod).

        - **assign_strand_ids** ([[`bool`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external}, optional) -- Automatically assign strand ID's to
          the created zones. This option is ignored if *concatenate* if
          [[`True`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/constants.html#True "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external}. (default: [[`True`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
          .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/constants.html#True "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
          .external})

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    :   A
        [generator](https://docs.python.org/3/reference/expressions.html#generator-expressions){.reference
        .external} of the extracted zones.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> slice_zone = plot.streamtraces.extract()
    :::
    ::::

:   Streamtrace line thickness.

    Line thickness as a percentage of the frame height for 2D lines, or
    a percentage of the median axis length for 3D surface lines and
    volume lines.

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[marker_symbol]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[symbol_type]{.pre}]{.n}[[=]{.pre}]{.o}[[None]{.pre}]{.default_value}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#Streamtraces.marker_symbol){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.marker_symbol "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Returns a streamline symbol style object.

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    :   **symbol_type** ([[`SymbolType`{.xref .any .py .py-class
        .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.SymbolType "tecplot.constant.SymbolType"){.reference
        .internal}, optional) -- The type of symbol to return. By
        default, this will return the active marker symbol type which is
        obtained from [[`Streamtraces.marker_symbol_type`{.xref .any .py
        .py-attr .docutils .literal

        .internal}.

    .internal}, depending on [[`marker_symbol_type`{.xref .any .py
    .py-attr .docutils .literal

    .internal}

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[max_steps]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.max_steps "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Maximum number of steps before the streamtrace is terminated.

    [[`max_steps`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.max_steps "tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.max_steps"){.reference
    .internal} prevents streamtraces from spinning forever in a vortex,
    or from wandering into a region where the vector components are very
    small, very random, or both.

    If a small [[`step_size`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.step_size "tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.step_size"){.reference
    .internal} is selected, the [[`max_steps`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.max_steps "tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.max_steps"){.reference
    .internal} should be a large value.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> plot.streamtraces.max_steps = 5000
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`int`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#int "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}

<!-- -->

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[min_step_size]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.min_step_size "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Smallest step size to use as a percentage of cell distance.

    A typical minimum step size value is 0.00001, which is the default.

    ::: {.admonition .warning}
    Warning

    Setting this too small results in integration problems. Setting this
    greater than or equal to the [[`step_size`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.step_size "tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.step_size"){.reference
    .internal} results in a constant step size.
    :::

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> plot.streamtraces.min_step_size = .0002
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`float`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}

<!-- -->

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[obey_source_zone_blanking]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.obey_source_zone_blanking "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Obey source zone blanking.

    When [[`True`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/constants.html#True "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
    .external}, streamtraces are generated for non-blanked regions only.
    When [[`False`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/constants.html#False "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
    .external}, streamtraces are generated for both blanked and
    unblanked regions.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> plot.streamtraces.obey_source_zone_blanking = True
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`bool`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[position]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[stream_number]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#Streamtraces.position){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.position "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Query the starting position of a streamtrace.

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    :   **stream_number** -- ([[`int`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#int "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}): 0-based stream number to query.

    Returns[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`tuple`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#tuple "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external} of [[`floats`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}

    Get the position of streamtrace number 3:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> position = plot.streamtraces.position(2) # Note: 0-based
        >>> position.x  # == position[0]
        0.1
        >>> position.y  # == position[1]
        0.2
        >>> position.z  # == position[2]
        0.3
    :::
    ::::

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[set_termination_line]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[line_points]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#Streamtraces.set_termination_line){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.set_termination_line "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Set the position of the termination line for streamtraces.

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    :   **line_points** -- (array of [[`float`{.xref .any .docutils
        .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external} [[`tuple`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/stdtypes.html#tuple "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}) Points of the termination line.

    Raises[:]{.colon}

    :   [**TecplotSystemError**](tecplot.exceptions.html#tecplot.exception.TecplotSystemError "tecplot.exception.TecplotSystemError"){.reference
        .internal} -- Termination line could not be set.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> # Multi-segment line between points (0,0)-(5,8)-(3,6)
        >>> line_points = [(0, 0), (5, 8), (3,6)]
        >>> plot.streamtraces.set_termination_line(line_points)
    :::
    ::::

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[step_size]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.step_size "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Maximum fraction of the distance across a cell that a streamtrace
    moves in one step.

    The step size is the maximum fraction of the distance across a cell
    that a streamtrace moves in one step. A streamtrace adjusts its step
    size between [[`step_size`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.step_size "tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.step_size"){.reference
    .internal} and [[`min_step_size`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.min_step_size "tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.min_step_size"){.reference
    .internal}, depending on local curvature of the streamtrace.

    A typical value (and the default) is **0.25**, which results in four
    integration steps through each cell or element. The value for Step
    Size affects the accuracy of the integration.

    ::: {.admonition .warning}
    Warning

    Setting step size too small can result in round-off errors, while
    setting it too large can result in truncation errors and missed
    cells.
    :::

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> plot.streamtraces.step_size = .25
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`float`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}

<!-- -->

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[streamtrace_type]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[stream_number]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#Streamtraces.streamtrace_type){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.streamtrace_type "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Query the type of a streamtrace by streamtrace number.

    Parameters[:]{.colon}

    :   **stream_number** -- ([[`int`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#int "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}): 0-based stream number to query.

    Returns[:]{.colon}

        .internal}

        >>> streamtrace_type
        <Streamtrace.VolumeLine: 2>
    :::
    ::::

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[termination_line]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.termination_line "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Streamtraces termination line attributes.

    A streamtrace termination line is a polyline that terminates any
    streamtraces that cross it. The termination line is useful for
    stopping streamtraces before they spiral or stall.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> term_line = plot.streamtraces.termination_line
        >>> term_line.show = True
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`StreamtraceTerminationLine`{.xref .any .py .py-class
        .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTerminationLine "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTerminationLine"){.reference
        .internal}

<!-- -->

[[Streamtraces.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[use_slice_clipping]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.Streamtraces.use_slice_clipping "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Clip isosurface by any intersecting slices.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> from tecplot.constant import ClipPlane
        >>> slice = plot.slice(0)
        >>> slice.clip = ClipPlane.AbovePrimarySlice
        >>> plot.fieldmap(0).effects.clip_planes = slice
        >>> plot.streamtraces.use_slice_clipping = True

::: {#streamtracerodribbon .section}
### [StreamtraceRodRibbon](#id129){.toc-backref role="doc-backlink"}[¶](#streamtracerodribbon "Link to this heading"){.headerlink}

*[class]{.pre}[ ]{.w}*[[tecplot.plot.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[StreamtraceRodRibbon]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[streamtrace]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#StreamtraceRodRibbon){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Get/Set streamtrace rod/ribbon attributes.

    The [[`StreamtraceRodRibbon`{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon"){.reference
    .internal} class allows you to query and set attributes of
    streamtrace rod/ribbon types:

    - [[`Streamtrace.SurfaceRibbon`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils
      .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.Streamtrace.SurfaceRibbon "tecplot.constant.Streamtrace.SurfaceRibbon"){.reference
      .internal}

    - [[`Streamtrace.VolumeRibbon`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils
      .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.Streamtrace.VolumeRibbon "tecplot.constant.Streamtrace.VolumeRibbon"){.reference
      .internal}

    - [[`Streamtrace.VolumeRod`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils
      .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.Streamtrace.VolumeRod "tecplot.constant.Streamtrace.VolumeRod"){.reference
      .internal}

    In addition to attributes common to all rod/ribbon streamtrace types
    such as [[`width`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.width "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.width"){.reference
    .internal}, some attributes are further divided into subcategories:

    - [[`Rod/ribbon`{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}` `{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils
      .literal .notranslate}[`mesh`{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils
      .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonMesh "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonMesh"){.reference
      .internal}

    - [[`Rod/ribbon`{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}` `{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils
      .literal .notranslate}[`contour`{.xref .any .py .py-class
      .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour"){.reference
      .internal}

    - [[`Rod/ribbon`{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}` `{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils
      .literal .notranslate}[`shade`{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils
      .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonShade "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonShade"){.reference
      .internal}

    - [[`Rod/ribbon`{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}` `{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils
      .literal .notranslate}[`effects`{.xref .any .py .py-class
      .docutils .literal
      .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonEffects "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonEffects"){.reference
      .internal}

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    To change the color of streamtrace rods/ribbons, set
    [[`StreamtraceRodRibbonShade.color`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonShade.color "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonShade.color"){.reference
    .internal}.
    :::

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        import os
        import tecplot
        from tecplot.constant import *

        examples_dir = tecplot.session.tecplot_examples_directory()
        datafile = os.path.join(examples_dir, 'SimpleData', 'DuctFlow.plt')
        dataset = tecplot.data.load_tecplot(datafile)

        frame = tecplot.active_frame()
        frame.plot_type = tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D

        plot = frame.plot()
        plot.show_mesh = False
        plot.show_shade = False
        plot.show_edge = True
        plot.fieldmap(0).edge.edge_type = EdgeType.Creases
        plot.contour(0).variable = dataset.variable(3)
        plot.contour(0).levels.reset_to_nice()

        plot.vector.u_variable_index = 3
        plot.vector.v_variable_index = 4
        plot.vector.w_variable_index = 5

        plot.show_streamtraces = True
        plot.streamtraces.rod_ribbon.width = .03
        plot.streamtraces.rod_ribbon.contour.show = True

        plot.streamtraces.add_rake(start_position=(1.5, 0.1, .4),
                                   end_position=(1.5, .9, 0.1),
                                   stream_type=Streamtrace.VolumeRibbon,
                                   num_seed_points=3)
        plot.streamtraces.add_rake(start_position=(1.5, 0.1, 0.1),
                                   end_position=(1.5, .9, .4),
                                   stream_type=Streamtrace.VolumeRod,
                                   num_seed_points=4)

        tecplot.export.save_png('streamtrace_ribbon.png', 600, supersample=3)
    :::
    ::::

    <figure class="align-default" style="width: 300px">
    <a href="../_images/streamtrace_ribbon.png"
    class="reference internal image-reference"><img
    src="../_images/streamtrace_ribbon.png" style="width: 300px;"
    alt="../_images/streamtrace_ribbon.png" /></a>
    </figure>

      ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- --------------------------------------------------------
      [[`contour`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.contour "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.contour"){.reference .internal}                        Streamtraces rod/ribbon contour attributes.
      [[`effects`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.effects "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.effects"){.reference .internal}                        Streamtraces rod/ribbon effects.
      [[`mesh`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.mesh "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.mesh"){.reference .internal}                                 Streamtraces rod/ribbon mesh attributes.
      [[`num_rod_points`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.num_rod_points "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.num_rod_points"){.reference .internal}   Number of rod points.
      [[`shade`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.shade "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.shade"){.reference .internal}                              Streamtraces rod/ribbon color and lighting attributes.
      [[`width`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.width "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.width"){.reference .internal}                              Rod/ribbon width in grid units.
      ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- --------------------------------------------------------

[[StreamtraceRodRibbon.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[num_rod_points]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbon.num_rod_points "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Number of rod points.

    Volume rods have a polygonal cross-section; this parameter tells
    [Tecplot
    360](https://www.tecplot.com/products/tecplot-360){.reference
    .external} what that cross-section should be. (Three is an
    equilateral triangle, four is a square, five is a regular pentagon,
    and so on.) If you want two sets of volume rods with different
    cross-sections, you must create one set and then extract the set as
    a zone, then configure a new set of streamtraces with the second
    cross-section.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> plot.streamtraces.rod_ribbon.num_rod_points = 10
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`int`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#int "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}, valid range 3-100

::: {#streamtracetiming .section}
### [StreamtraceTiming](#id130){.toc-backref role="doc-backlink"}[¶](#streamtracetiming "Link to this heading"){.headerlink}

*[class]{.pre}[ ]{.w}*[[tecplot.plot.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[StreamtraceTiming]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[streamtrace]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#StreamtraceTiming){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Timed markers for streamlines.

    Use [[`StreamtraceTiming`{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming"){.reference
    .internal} to control timed markers for streamlines, and timed
    dashes for all types of streamtraces. Stream markers are drawn at
    time locations along streamlines. The spacing between stream markers
    is proportional to the magnitude of the local vector field:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        import tecplot
        from tecplot.constant import *
        import os

        examples_dir = tecplot.session.tecplot_examples_directory()
        datafile = os.path.join(examples_dir, 'SimpleData', 'VortexShedding.plt')
        dataset = tecplot.data.load_tecplot(datafile)

        frame = tecplot.active_frame()
        frame.plot_type = tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian2D

        plot = frame.plot()
        plot.vector.u_variable = dataset.variable('U(M/S)')
        plot.vector.v_variable = dataset.variable('V(M/S)')
        plot.show_streamtraces = True
        plot.show_shade = True
        plot.fieldmap(0).shade.color = Color.LightBlue


        streamtraces = plot.streamtraces
        streamtraces.show_markers = True
        timing = streamtraces.timing
        timing.anchor = 0
        timing.delta = 0.0001

        streamtraces.marker_size = 1.5
        streamtraces.marker_symbol().shape =GeomShape.RTri
        streamtraces.marker_color = Color.Mahogany

        streamtraces.add_rake(start_position=(-0.003, 0.005),
                              end_position=(-0.003, -0.005),
                              stream_type=Streamtrace.TwoDLine,
                              num_seed_points=10)


        plot.axes.y_axis.min = -0.02
        plot.axes.y_axis.max = 0.02
        plot.axes.x_axis.min = -0.008
        plot.axes.x_axis.max = 0.04

        tecplot.export.save_png('streamtrace_timing.png', 600, supersample=3)
    :::
    ::::

    <figure class="align-default" style="width: 300px">
    <a href="../_images/streamtrace_timing.png"
    class="reference internal image-reference"><img
    src="../_images/streamtrace_timing.png" style="width: 300px;"
    alt="../_images/streamtrace_timing.png" /></a>
    </figure>

    **Attributes**

      ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- -------------------------------------------------
      [[`anchor`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.anchor "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.anchor"){.reference .internal}   Time that a dash is guaranteed to start.
      [[`delta`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.delta "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.delta"){.reference .internal}      Time between stream markers.
      [[`end`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.end "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.end"){.reference .internal}            Time after which no stream markers are drawn.
      [[`start`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.start "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.start"){.reference .internal}      Time at which the first marker should be drawn.
      ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- -------------------------------------------------

    **Methods**

      ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- -----------------------------------------------
      [[`reset_delta`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.reset_delta "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.reset_delta"){.reference .internal}()   Reset the time delta for dashed streamtraces.
      ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- -----------------------------------------------

<!-- -->

[[StreamtraceTiming.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[anchor]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.anchor "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Time that a dash is guaranteed to start.

    A dash is guaranteed to start at [[`anchor`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.anchor "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.anchor"){.reference
    .internal}, provided the start and end time surround the dash.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> plot.streamtraces.timing.anchor = 1.1
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`float`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}

<!-- -->

[[StreamtraceTiming.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[delta]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.delta "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Time between stream markers.

    [[`delta`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.delta "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.delta"){.reference
    .internal} is the time interval that measures the time between
    stream markers. The actual distance between markers is the product
    of this number and the local [[`Vector`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.PrintRenderType.Vector "tecplot.constant.PrintRenderType.Vector"){.reference
    .internal} magnitude.

    Call [[`StreamtraceTiming.reset_delta()`{.xref .any .py .py-meth
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.reset_delta "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.reset_delta"){.reference
    .internal} to reset this to the default.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> plot.streamtraces.timing.delta = 0.1
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`float`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}

<!-- -->

[[StreamtraceTiming.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[end]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.end "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Time after which no stream markers are drawn.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> plot.streamtraces.timing.end = 3.0
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`float`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}

<!-- -->

[[StreamtraceTiming.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[reset_delta]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#StreamtraceTiming.reset_delta){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.reset_delta "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Reset the time delta for dashed streamtraces.

    The delta time is reset such that a stream dash in the vicinity of
    the maximum vector magnitude will have a length approximately equal
    to 10 percent of the frame width.

    Raises[:]{.colon}

    :   [**TecplotSystemError**](tecplot.exceptions.html#tecplot.exception.TecplotSystemError "tecplot.exception.TecplotSystemError"){.reference
        .internal} -- Streamtraces time delta could not be reset.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> plot.streamtraces.timing.reset_delta()
    :::
    ::::

<!-- -->

[[StreamtraceTiming.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[start]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceTiming.start "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Time at which the first marker should be drawn.

    A start time of zero means that the first marker is drawn at the
    starting point. A start time of 2.5 means that the first stream
    marker is drawn 2.5 time units downstream of the starting point.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> plot.streamtraces.timing.start = 2.5
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`float`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#float "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}
:::

::: {#streamtracerodribboncontour .section}
### [StreamtraceRodRibbonContour](#id131){.toc-backref role="doc-backlink"}[¶](#streamtracerodribboncontour "Link to this heading"){.headerlink}

*[class]{.pre}[ ]{.w}*[[tecplot.plot.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[StreamtraceRodRibbonContour]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[streamtrace]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#StreamtraceRodRibbonContour){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Contour flooding display for streamtrace rod/ribbons.

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        import os
        import numpy as np
        import tecplot
        from tecplot.constant import *

        examples_dir = tecplot.session.tecplot_examples_directory()
        datafile = os.path.join(examples_dir, 'SimpleData', 'DownDraft.plt')
        dataset = tecplot.data.load_tecplot(datafile)

        frame = tecplot.active_frame()
        frame.plot_type = tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D

        plot = frame.plot()
        plot.fieldmap(0).surfaces.surfaces_to_plot = SurfacesToPlot.BoundaryFaces
        plot.contour(0).variable = dataset.variable(3)
        plot.contour(0).levels.reset_levels(np.linspace(1.15,1.25,11))
        plot.show_mesh = False
        plot.show_shade = False
        plot.show_edge = True

        plot.vector.u_variable_index = 4
        plot.vector.v_variable_index = 5
        plot.vector.w_variable_index = 6
        plot.show_streamtraces = True

        rod = plot.streamtraces.rod_ribbon
        rod.width = .008
        rod.contour.show = True
        rod.contour.use_lighting_effect = True

        plot.streamtraces.add_rake(
            start_position=(0, 0.22, 0),
            end_position=(0, 0.22, 0.1),
            stream_type=Streamtrace.VolumeRod)

        plot.view.width = 0.644
        plot.view.alpha = 66.4
        plot.view.theta = -122.4
        plot.view.psi   = 124.5
        plot.view.position = (5.3, 3.56, -4.3)

        tecplot.export.save_png('streamtrace_rod_contour.png', 600, supersample=3)
    :::
    ::::

    <figure class="align-default" style="width: 300px">
    <a href="../_images/streamtrace_rod_contour.png"
    class="reference internal image-reference"><img
    src="../_images/streamtrace_rod_contour.png" style="width: 300px;"
    alt="../_images/streamtrace_rod_contour.png" /></a>
    </figure>

    **Attributes**

      ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      [[`flood_contour_group`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.flood_contour_group "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.flood_contour_group"){.reference .internal}                     Contour group to use for flooding.
      [[`flood_contour_group_index`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.flood_contour_group_index "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.flood_contour_group_index"){.reference .internal}   The [[`Index`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.LabelType.Index "tecplot.constant.LabelType.Index"){.reference .internal} of the [[`ContourGroup`{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.ContourGroup "tecplot.plot.ContourGroup"){.reference .internal} to use for flooding.
      [[`show`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.show "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.show"){.reference .internal}                                                                  Enable or disable contour flooding display.
      [[`use_lighting_effect`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.use_lighting_effect "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.use_lighting_effect"){.reference .internal}                     Enable lighting effect for streamtrace rod/ribbons.
      ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

<!-- -->

[[StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[flood_contour_group]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.flood_contour_group "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Contour group to use for flooding.

    This property sets and gets the ContourGroup used for flooding.
    Changing style on this [[`ContourGroup`{.xref .any .py .py-class
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.ContourGroup "tecplot.plot.ContourGroup"){.reference
    .internal} will affect all fieldmaps on the same [[`Frame`{.xref
    .any .py .py-class .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.layout.html#tecplot.layout.Frame "tecplot.layout.Frame"){.reference
    .internal} that use it.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> group = plot.contour(1)
        >>> contour = plot.streamtraces.rod_ribbon.contour
        >>> contour.flood_contour_group = group
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`ContourGroup`{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.ContourGroup "tecplot.plot.ContourGroup"){.reference
        .internal}

<!-- -->

[[StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[flood_contour_group_index]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.flood_contour_group_index "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   The [[`Index`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.LabelType.Index "tecplot.constant.LabelType.Index"){.reference
    .internal} of the [[`ContourGroup`{.xref .any .py .py-class
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.ContourGroup "tecplot.plot.ContourGroup"){.reference
    .internal} to use for flooding.

    This property sets and gets, by [[`Index`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.LabelType.Index "tecplot.constant.LabelType.Index"){.reference
    .internal}, the [[`ContourGroup`{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils
    .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.ContourGroup "tecplot.plot.ContourGroup"){.reference
    .internal} used for flooding. Changing style on this
    [[`ContourGroup`{.xref .any .py .py-class .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.ContourGroup "tecplot.plot.ContourGroup"){.reference
    .internal} will affect all fieldmaps on the same [[`Frame`{.xref
    .any .py .py-class .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.layout.html#tecplot.layout.Frame "tecplot.layout.Frame"){.reference
    .internal} that use it.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> contour = plot.streamtraces.rod_ribbon.contour
        >>> contour.flood_contour_group_index = 0  # First contour group
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`Index`{.xref .any .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.LabelType.Index "tecplot.constant.LabelType.Index"){.reference
        .internal} (zero-based index)

<!-- -->

[[StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[show]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.show "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Enable or disable contour flooding display.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> plot.streamtraces.rod_ribbon.contour.show = True
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`bool`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}

<!-- -->

[[StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[use_lighting_effect]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[¶](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.use_lighting_effect "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Enable lighting effect for streamtrace rod/ribbons.

    ::: {.admonition .note}
    Note

    Setting [[`StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.use_lighting_effect`{.xref
    .any .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.use_lighting_effect "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonContour.use_lighting_effect"){.reference
    .internal} will also set the same value for
    [[`StreamtraceRodRibbonShade.use_lighting_effect`{.xref .any .py
    .py-attr .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonShade.use_lighting_effect "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonShade.use_lighting_effect"){.reference
    .internal}, and vice-versa.
    :::

    The lighting effect is set with
    [[`StreamtraceRodRibbonEffects.lighting_effect`{.xref .any .py
    .py-attr .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonEffects.lighting_effect "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonEffects.lighting_effect"){.reference
    .internal}, and may be one of [[`LightingEffect.Gouraud`{.xref .any
    .py .py-attr .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.LightingEffect.Gouraud "tecplot.constant.LightingEffect.Gouraud"){.reference
    .internal} or [[`LightingEffect.Paneled`{.xref .any .py .py-attr
    .docutils .literal
    .notranslate}]{.pre}](tecplot.constants.html#tecplot.constant.LightingEffect.Paneled "tecplot.constant.LightingEffect.Paneled"){.reference
    .internal}.

    Example usage:

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        >>> ribbon = plot.streamtraces.rod_ribbon
        >>> contour = ribbon.contour
        >>> contour.use_lighting_effect = True
        >>> ribbon.effects.lighting_effect = LightingEffect.Paneled
    :::
    ::::

    Type[:]{.colon}

    :   [[`bool`{.xref .any .docutils .literal
        .notranslate}]{.pre}](https://docs.python.org/3/library/functions.html#bool "(in Python v3.13)"){.reference
        .external}
:::

::: {#streamtracerodribboneffects .section}
### [StreamtraceRodRibbonEffects](#id132){.toc-backref role="doc-backlink"}[¶](#streamtracerodribboneffects "Link to this heading"){.headerlink}

*[class]{.pre}[ ]{.w}*[[tecplot.plot.]{.pre}]{.sig-prename .descclassname}[[StreamtraceRodRibbonEffects]{.pre}]{.sig-name .descname}[(]{.sig-paren}*[[streamtrace]{.pre}]{.n}*[)]{.sig-paren}[[[\[source\]]{.pre}]{.viewcode-link}](../_modules/tecplot/plot/streamtrace.html#StreamtraceRodRibbonEffects){.reference .internal}[¶](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonEffects "Link to this definition"){.headerlink}

:   Controls how lighting and translucency interacts with streamtrace
    rods and ribbons.

    :::: {.highlight-python .notranslate}
    ::: highlight
        import os
        import tecplot
        from tecplot.constant import *

        examples_dir = tecplot.session.tecplot_examples_directory()
        datafile = os.path.join(examples_dir, 'SimpleData', 'DuctFlow.plt')
        dataset = tecplot.data.load_tecplot(datafile)

        frame = tecplot.active_frame()
        frame.plot_type = tecplot.constant.PlotType.Cartesian3D

        plot = frame.plot()
        plot.show_mesh = False
        plot.show_shade = False
        plot.show_edge = True
        plot.fieldmap(0).edge.edge_type = EdgeType.Creases

        plot.show_mesh = False
        plot.show_shade = False

        plot.vector.u_variable_index = 3
        plot.vector.v_variable_index = 4
        plot.vector.w_variable_index = 5
        plot.show_streamtraces = True
        plot.streamtraces.rod_ribbon.width = .03
        plot.streamtraces.rod_ribbon.shade.color = Color.Green

        plot.streamtraces.rod_ribbon.effects.use_translucency = True
        plot.streamtraces.rod_ribbon.effects.surface_translucency = 80

        plot.streamtraces.add_rake(start_position=(1.5, 0, .45),
                                       end_position=(1.5, 1, 0),
                                       stream_type=Streamtrace.VolumeRibbon)

        tecplot.export.save_png('streamtrace_ribbon_effects.png', 600, supersample=3)
    :::
    ::::

    <figure class="align-default" style="width: 300px">

    class="reference internal image-reference"><img
    src="../_images/streamtrace_ribbon_effects.png" style="width: 300px;"
    alt="../_images/streamtrace_ribbon_effects.png" /></a>
    </figure>

      [[`surface_translucency`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonEffects.surface_translucency "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonEffects.surface_translucency"){.reference .internal}   Surface translucency of the streamtraces ribbon.
      [[`use_translucency`{.xref .py .py-obj .docutils .literal .notranslate}]{.pre}](#tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonEffects.use_translucency "tecplot.plot.StreamtraceRodRibbonEffects.use_translucency"){.reference .internal}               Enable surface translucency.
